# NuXJS Benchmark Bytecode Disassembly

This report captures the NuXJS bytecode generated for the benchmark suite and summarises opcode usage patterns.
The disassembly data was collected with `dasm()` through the NuXJS REPL while suppressing benchmark printing side-effects.
